| 4 | 473.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | companies (other than Small Finance Banks, Payments Banks, and Local Area |

| 4 | 494.3 | 100.4 | 523.3 | Banks), corresponding new banks, and the State Bank of India, as defined |

| 6 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 6 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 6 | 79.7 | 73.0 | 162.4 | C. Definitions |

| 6 | 111.8 | 73.0 | 523.3 | 5. The following definitions are sourced from FSB Cyber Lexicon unless explicitly |

| 6 | 132.5 | 100.4 | 523.3 | mentioned otherwise. In these Directions, unless the context states otherwise, |

| 6 | 153.2 | 100.4 | 449.8 | the terms herein shall bear the meanings assigned to them below. |

| 6 | 185.9 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (1) ‘Audit Trail’ - A chronological record that reconstructs and examines the |

| 6 | 206.6 | 128.7 | 523.2 | sequence of activities surrounding or leading to a specific operation, |

| 6 | 227.3 | 128.7 | 523.3 | procedure, or event in a security-relevant transaction from inception to |

| 6 | 248.0 | 128.7 | 161.4 | result. |

| 6 | 280.7 | 128.7 | 523.4 | (Source: NIST SP 800-53r5 on Security and Privacy Controls for |

| 6 | 301.4 | 128.7 | 342.7 | Information Systems and Organizations) |

| 6 | 334.1 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (2) ‘Availability’ - Property of being accessible and usable on demand by an |

| 6 | 354.8 | 128.7 | 219.2 | authorised entity. |

| 6 | 387.5 | 100.3 | 523.2 | (3) ‘Confidentiality’ - Property that information is neither made available nor |

| 6 | 408.2 | 128.7 | 498.3 | disclosed to unauthorised individuals, entities, processes, or systems. |

| 6 | 440.9 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (4) ‘Cyber’ - Relating to, within, or through the medium of the interconnected |

| 6 | 461.6 | 128.7 | 523.3 | information infrastructure of interactions among persons, processes, data, |

| 6 | 482.3 | 128.7 | 262.1 | and information systems. |

| 6 | 515.0 | 100.3 | 523.2 | (5) ‘Cyber Event’ – Any observable occurrence in an information system. Cyber |

| 6 | 535.7 | 128.7 | 500.9 | events sometimes provide indication that a cyber incident is occurring. |

| 6 | 568.4 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (6) ‘Cybersecurity’ - Preservation of confidentiality, integrity, and availability of |

| 6 | 589.0 | 128.7 | 523.4 | information and / or information systems through the cyber medium. In |

| 6 | 609.7 | 128.7 | 523.3 | addition, other properties, such as authenticity, accountability, non- |

| 6 | 630.4 | 128.7 | 380.2 | repudiation, and reliability can also be involved. |

| 6 | 663.1 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (7) ‘Cyber Incident’ - A cyber event that adversely affects the cybersecurity of |

| 6 | 683.8 | 128.7 | 489.5 | an information asset whether resulting from malicious activity or not. |

| 6 | 716.5 | 128.7 | 523.3 | (Source: Cyber incident definition is adapted from FSB Cyber Lexicon. By |

| 6 | 737.2 | 128.7 | 506.0 | the definition, it includes cybersecurity incidents as well as IT incidents) |

| 6 | 807.4 | 294.8 | 300.4 | 6 |

| 7 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 7 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 7 | 79.1 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (8) ‘Cyber Resilience’ - The ability of an organisation to continue to carry out |

| 7 | 99.8 | 128.7 | 523.3 | its mission by anticipating and adapting to cyber threats and other relevant |

| 7 | 120.5 | 128.7 | 523.3 | changes in the environment and by withstanding, containing, and rapidly |

| 7 | 141.2 | 128.7 | 298.7 | recovering from cyber incidents. |

| 7 | 173.9 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (9) ‘Cyber-attack’ - Malicious attempt(s) to exploit vulnerabilities through the |

| 7 | 194.6 | 128.7 | 518.3 | cyber medium to damage, disrupt, or gain unauthorised access to assets. |

| 7 | 227.3 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (10) ‘Cyber Threat’ - A circumstance with the potential to exploit one or more |

| 7 | 248.0 | 128.7 | 394.3 | vulnerabilities that adversely affects cybersecurity. |

| 7 | 280.7 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (11) ‘Data Dictionary’ - A description of data in business terms, including |

| 7 | 301.4 | 128.7 | 523.3 | information about the data. It includes elements like data types, structure |

| 7 | 322.1 | 128.7 | 299.4 | details, and security restrictions. |

| 7 | 354.8 | 128.7 | 264.8 | (Source: ISACA glossary) |

| 7 | 387.5 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (12) ‘De-militarized Zone’ or ‘DMZ’ - A perimeter network segment that is |

| 7 | 408.2 | 128.7 | 386.8 | logically between internal and external networks. |

| 7 | 440.9 | 128.7 | 305.4 | (Source: NIST SP 800-82 Rev. 2) |

| 7 | 473.6 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (13) ‘Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS)’ - A denial of service that is carried |

| 7 | 494.3 | 128.7 | 364.6 | out using numerous sources simultaneously. |

| 7 | 527.0 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (14) ‘Digital Forensics’ - The process used to acquire, preserve, analyse, and |

| 7 | 547.7 | 128.7 | 523.2 | report on evidence using scientific methods that are demonstrably reliable, |

| 7 | 568.4 | 128.7 | 264.7 | accurate, and repeatable. |

| 7 | 601.0 | 128.7 | 523.3 | (Source: adapted from NIST Cloud Computing Forensic Science |

| 7 | 621.7 | 128.7 | 192.8 | Challenges) |

| 7 | 654.4 | 100.3 | 523.2 | (15) ‘Framework’ - A structured set of strategies, policies, processes, methods, |

| 7 | 675.1 | 128.7 | 523.3 | and best practices that guides organisational activities, enables |

| 7 | 695.8 | 128.7 | 523.3 | governance and control, and supports the achievement of defined |

| 7 | 716.5 | 128.7 | 185.3 | objectives. |

| 7 | 749.2 | 128.7 | 452.9 | (Source: adapted from ISACA glossary and ISO 22340:2024) |

| 7 | 807.4 | 294.8 | 300.4 | 7 |

| 8 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 8 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 8 | 79.1 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (16) ‘Honeypot’ - A specially configured server, also known as a decoy server, |

| 8 | 99.8 | 128.7 | 523.3 | designed to attract and monitor intruders in a manner so that their actions |

| 8 | 120.5 | 128.7 | 304.6 | do not affect production systems. |

| 8 | 153.2 | 128.7 | 264.8 | (Source: ISACA glossary) |

| 8 | 185.9 | 100.3 | 523.2 | (17) ‘Indicators of Compromise (IOCs)’ - Identifying signs that a cyber incident |

| 8 | 206.6 | 128.7 | 392.7 | may have occurred or may be currently occurring. |

| 8 | 239.3 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (18) ‘Information Asset’ - Any piece of data, device, or other component of the |

| 8 | 260.0 | 128.7 | 523.3 | environment that supports information-related activities. Information Assets |

| 8 | 280.7 | 128.7 | 436.2 | include information system, data, hardware, and software. |

| 8 | 313.4 | 128.7 | 523.3 | (Source: Information Asset definition is adapted from “Guidance on cyber |

| 8 | 334.1 | 128.7 | 523.3 | resilience for financial market infrastructures” publication of Bank for |

| 8 | 354.8 | 128.7 | 523.3 | International Settlements and International Organization of Securities |

| 8 | 375.5 | 128.7 | 277.4 | Commissions of June 2016) |

| 8 | 408.2 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (19) ‘Information System’ - Set of applications, services, information technology |

| 8 | 428.9 | 128.7 | 523.3 | assets, or other information-handling components, which includes the |

| 8 | 449.6 | 128.7 | 326.1 | operating environment and networks. |

| 8 | 482.3 | 100.3 | 401.0 | (20) ‘Integrity’ - Property of accuracy and completeness. |

| 8 | 515.0 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (21) ‘IT Governance’ - The responsibility of executives and the board of |

| 8 | 535.7 | 128.7 | 523.3 | directors; consists of the leadership, organisational structures, and |

| 8 | 556.4 | 128.7 | 523.3 | processes that ensure that the enterprise’s IT sustains and extends the |

| 8 | 577.0 | 128.7 | 329.1 | enterprise's strategies and objectives. |

| 8 | 609.7 | 128.7 | 328.1 | (Source: ISACA glossary and COBIT) |

| 8 | 642.4 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (22) ‘IT Risk’ - The business risk associated with the use, ownership, operation, |

| 8 | 663.1 | 128.7 | 461.4 | involvement, influence, and adoption of IT within an enterprise. |

| 8 | 695.8 | 128.7 | 264.8 | (Source: ISACA glossary) |

| 8 | 807.4 | 294.8 | 300.4 | 8 |

| 9 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 9 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 9 | 79.1 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (23) ‘Malware’ - Software designed with malicious intent containing features or |

| 9 | 99.8 | 128.7 | 523.3 | capabilities that can potentially cause harm directly or indirectly to entities |

| 9 | 120.5 | 128.7 | 279.4 | or their information systems. |

| 9 | 153.2 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (24) ‘Penetration Testing’ - A test methodology in which assessors typically |

| 9 | 173.9 | 128.7 | 523.3 | working under specific constraints, attempt to circumvent or defeat the |

| 9 | 194.6 | 128.7 | 354.2 | security features of an information system. |

| 9 | 227.3 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (25) ‘Phishing’ - A digital form of social engineering that attempts to acquire |

| 9 | 248.0 | 128.7 | 523.3 | private or confidential information by pretending to be a trustworthy entity |

| 9 | 268.7 | 128.7 | 296.7 | in an electronic communication. |

| 9 | 301.4 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (26) ‘Privileged User’ - A user who, by virtue of function, and / or role, has been |

| 9 | 322.1 | 128.7 | 523.3 | allocated powers within an information system, which are significantly |

| 9 | 342.8 | 128.7 | 404.9 | greater than those available to the majority of users. |

| 9 | 375.5 | 128.7 | 376.8 | (Source: adapted from ISO/IEC 24775-2:2021) |

| 9 | 408.2 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (27) ‘Recovery Point Objective’ - The point in time to which data must be |

| 9 | 428.9 | 128.7 | 269.4 | recovered after an outage. |

| 9 | 461.6 | 128.7 | 256.5 | (Source: NIST glossary) |

| 9 | 494.3 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (28) ‘Recovery Time Objective’ - The overall length of time an information |

| 9 | 515.0 | 128.7 | 523.3 | system’s components can be in the recovery phase before negatively |

| 9 | 535.7 | 128.7 | 498.0 | impacting the organisation’s mission or mission / business processes. |

| 9 | 568.4 | 128.7 | 256.5 | (Source: NIST glossary) |

| 9 | 601.0 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (29) ‘Red Team’ - A group of people authorised and organised to emulate a |

| 9 | 621.7 | 128.7 | 523.3 | potential adversary’s attack or exploitation capabilities against an |

| 9 | 642.4 | 128.7 | 523.4 | enterprise’s security posture. The Red Team’s objective is to improve |

| 9 | 663.1 | 128.7 | 523.3 | enterprise cybersecurity by demonstrating the impacts of successful |

| 9 | 683.8 | 128.7 | 523.3 | attacks and by demonstrating what works for the defenders (i.e., the Blue |

| 9 | 704.5 | 128.7 | 326.9 | Team) in an operational environment. |

| 9 | 737.2 | 128.7 | 256.5 | (Source: NIST glossary) |

| 9 | 807.4 | 294.8 | 300.4 | 9 |

| 10 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 10 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 10 | 79.1 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (30) ‘Red Teaming Exercise’ - An exercise, reflecting real-world conditions, that |

| 10 | 99.8 | 128.7 | 523.3 | is conducted as a simulated adversarial attempt by a red team to |

| 10 | 120.5 | 128.7 | 523.3 | compromise organisational missions and / or business processes to |

| 10 | 141.2 | 128.7 | 523.4 | provide a comprehensive assessment of the security capability of the |

| 10 | 161.9 | 128.7 | 324.8 | information system and organisation. |

| 10 | 194.6 | 128.7 | 330.6 | (Source: adapted from NIST glossary) |

| 10 | 227.3 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (31) ‘Vulnerability’ - A weakness, susceptibility, or flaw of an asset or control that |

| 10 | 248.0 | 128.7 | 343.5 | can be exploited by one or more threats. |

| 10 | 280.7 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (32) ‘Vulnerability Assessment (VA)’ - Systematic examination of an information |

| 10 | 301.4 | 128.7 | 523.3 | system or product to determine the adequacy of security measures, identify |

| 10 | 322.1 | 128.7 | 523.3 | security deficiencies, provide data from which to predict the effectiveness |

| 10 | 342.8 | 128.7 | 523.3 | of proposed security measures and confirm the adequacy of such |

| 10 | 363.5 | 128.7 | 296.8 | measures after implementation. |

| 10 | 396.2 | 63.0 | 523.3 | 6. All other expressions unless defined herein shall have the same meaning as have |

| 10 | 416.9 | 90.0 | 523.3 | been assigned to them under the Reserve Bank of India Act, 1934, the Banking |

| 10 | 437.6 | 90.0 | 523.3 | Regulation Act, 1949, the Information Technology Act, 2000, the Companies Act, |

| 10 | 458.3 | 90.0 | 523.3 | 2013 or any statutory modification or re-enactment thereto or other regulations |

| 10 | 479.0 | 90.0 | 523.3 | issued by the RBI or the Glossary of Terms published by the RBI or as used in |

| 10 | 499.7 | 90.0 | 314.8 | commercial parlance, as the case may be. |

| 11 | 79.7 | 214.6 | 380.6 | Chapter II - Role of the Board |

| 11 | 112.4 | 73.0 | 243.1 | A. Board Approved Policies |

| 11 | 144.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 7. The Board of Directors shall approve the strategies and policies related to |

| 11 | 165.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | Information Technology, Information Assets, Business Continuity, Information |

| 11 | 185.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | Security and Cybersecurity (including Incident Response and Recovery |

| 11 | 206.6 | 100.4 | 391.8 | Management / Cyber Crisis Management) frameworks. |

| 11 | 239.3 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 8. The Board may delegate the review of such policies and strategies to the Board- |

| 11 | 260.0 | 100.4 | 523.3 | level IT Strategy Committee (ITSC). Material amendments thereto shall be |

| 11 | 279.4 | 100.4 | 493.4 | approved by the Board. The review shall be undertaken at least annually1. |

| 11 | 314.0 | 73.0 | 241.1 | B. Board Level Committees |

| 11 | 346.1 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 9. The bank shall put in place a Board-level IT Strategy Committee (ITSC). The |

| 11 | 366.8 | 100.4 | 523.3 | composition and functions of the Committee shall be in accordance with |

| 11 | 387.5 | 100.4 | 283.8 | paragraphs 16 to 19 of Chapter III. |

| 11 | 420.2 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 10. The Audit Committee of the Board (ACB) shall be responsible for exercising |

| 11 | 440.9 | 100.4 | 273.2 | oversight of IS Audit of the bank. |

| 11 | 748.4 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 1 Substituted w.e.f. October 1, 2026, consequent to Reserve Bank of India (Commercial Banks - |

| 11 | 761.1 | 72.0 | 264.4 | Governance) Amendment Directions, 2026. |

| 11 | 807.4 | 292.4 | 302.8 | 11 |

| 12 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 12 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 12 | 79.7 | 115.4 | 479.9 | Chapter III – Information Technology Governance and Oversight |

| 12 | 112.4 | 72.0 | 251.1 | A. IT Governance Framework |

| 12 | 144.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 11. The key focus areas of IT Governance shall include strategic alignment, risk |

| 12 | 165.2 | 100.4 | 523.4 | management, resource management, performance management, and Business |

| 12 | 185.9 | 100.4 | 257.7 | Continuity / DR Management. |

| 12 | 218.6 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 12. The bank shall put in place a robust IT Governance Framework based on the |

| 12 | 239.3 | 100.4 | 410.3 | focus areas outlined in Paragraph 11 above, that inter alia: |

| 12 | 272.0 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (1) specifies the governance structure and processes necessary to meet the |

| 12 | 292.7 | 128.7 | 329.3 | bank’s business / strategic objectives; |

| 12 | 325.4 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (2) specifies the roles (including authority) and responsibilities of the Board of |

| 12 | 346.1 | 128.7 | 513.0 | Directors (Board) / Board level Committee and Senior Management; and |

| 12 | 378.8 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (3) includes adequate oversight mechanisms to ensure accountability and |

| 12 | 399.5 | 128.7 | 408.6 | mitigation of IT and cyber / information security risks. |

| 12 | 432.2 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 13. Enterprise-wide risk management policy or operational risk management policy |

| 12 | 452.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | shall also incorporate periodic assessment of IT-related risks (both inherent and |

| 12 | 473.6 | 100.4 | 175.1 | potential risk). |

| 12 | 506.9 | 72.0 | 402.5 | B. Information Security Policy and Cybersecurity Policy |

| 12 | 539.0 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 14. The bank shall put in place an Information Security Policy that takes into |

| 12 | 559.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | consideration, inter alia, aspects such as the objectives, scope, ownership, and |

| 12 | 580.4 | 100.4 | 523.2 | responsibility for the policy; information security organisational structure; |

| 12 | 601.0 | 100.4 | 523.4 | exceptions; compliance review and penal measures for non-compliance of |

| 12 | 621.7 | 100.4 | 143.7 | policies. |

| 12 | 654.4 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 15. The bank shall put in place a Cybersecurity Policy elucidating the strategy |

| 12 | 675.1 | 100.4 | 523.4 | containing an appropriate approach to combat cyber threats given the level of |

| 12 | 695.8 | 100.4 | 523.3 | complexity of business and acceptable levels of risk, duly approved by its Board. |

| 12 | 716.5 | 100.4 | 523.6 | The cybersecurity policy shall be distinct and separate from the broader IT |

| 12 | 737.2 | 100.4 | 523.4 | policy / Information Security policy so that it can highlight the risks from cyber |

| 12 | 757.9 | 100.4 | 411.8 | threats and the measures to address / mitigate these risks. |

| 12 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 12 |

| 13 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 13 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 13 | 79.7 | 72.0 | 301.7 | C. IT Strategy Committee of the Board |

| 13 | 111.8 | 72.0 | 468.3 | 16. The bank shall establish a Board-level IT Strategy Committee (ITSC). |

| 13 | 144.5 | 72.0 | 443.1 | 17. While constituting the ITSC, the bank shall ensure the following - |

| 13 | 177.2 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (1) ITSC has minimum of three directors (including the Chairperson of the |

| 13 | 197.9 | 128.7 | 232.7 | ITSC) as members; |

| 13 | 230.6 | 128.7 | 523.3 | (Note: Foreign banks operating in India through branch mode may leverage |

| 13 | 251.3 | 128.7 | 523.2 | upon controlling office / head office / regional / zonal Committees for |

| 13 | 272.0 | 128.7 | 523.2 | compliance with this Master Direction as long as governance obligations / |

| 13 | 292.7 | 128.7 | 465.5 | responsibilities outlined for the prescribed committees are met.) |

| 13 | 325.4 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (2) The Chairperson of the ITSC is an independent director having substantial |

| 13 | 346.1 | 128.7 | 523.3 | IT expertise in managing / guiding information technology initiatives; |

| 13 | 366.8 | 128.7 | 523.8 | ‘Substantial IT expertise’ means having a minimum of seven years’ |

| 13 | 387.5 | 128.7 | 523.3 | experience in managing information systems and / or leading / guiding |

| 13 | 408.2 | 128.7 | 523.3 | technology/ cybersecurity initiatives / projects. The Chairperson should also |

| 13 | 428.9 | 128.7 | 523.5 | understand the business processes at a broader level and the impact of IT |

| 13 | 449.6 | 128.7 | 232.1 | on such processes. |

| 13 | 482.3 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (3) Members are technically competent. Technically competent herein shall |

| 13 | 503.0 | 128.7 | 523.4 | mean the ability to understand and evaluate information systems and |

| 13 | 523.7 | 128.7 | 270.5 | associated IT / cyber risks. |

| 13 | 556.4 | 72.0 | 363.0 | 18. The ITSC shall meet at least on a quarterly basis. |

| 13 | 589.0 | 72.0 | 183.0 | 19. The ITSC shall: |

| 13 | 621.7 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (1) ensure that the bank has put an effective IT strategic planning process in |

| 13 | 642.4 | 128.7 | 160.7 | place; |

| 13 | 675.1 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (2) guide in preparation of IT Strategy and ensure that the IT Strategy aligns |

| 13 | 695.8 | 128.7 | 523.3 | with the overall strategy of the bank towards accomplishment of its business |

| 13 | 716.5 | 128.7 | 185.3 | objectives; |

| 13 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 13 |

| 14 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 14 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 14 | 79.1 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (3) satisfy itself that the IT Governance and Information Security Governance |

| 14 | 99.8 | 128.7 | 523.3 | structure fosters accountability, is effective and efficient, has adequate |

| 14 | 120.5 | 128.7 | 523.3 | skilled resources, well - defined objectives, and unambiguous |

| 14 | 141.2 | 128.7 | 386.8 | responsibilities for each level in the organisation; |

| 14 | 173.9 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (4) ensure that the bank has put in place processes for assessing and |

| 14 | 194.6 | 128.7 | 323.9 | managing IT and cybersecurity risks; |

| 14 | 227.3 | 100.3 | 523.5 | (5) ensure that the budgetary allocations for the IT function, including IT |

| 14 | 248.0 | 128.7 | 523.3 | security / cybersecurity, are commensurate with the bank’s IT maturity, |

| 14 | 268.7 | 128.7 | 523.3 | digital depth, threat environment, and industry standards and are utilised in |

| 14 | 289.4 | 128.7 | 408.2 | a manner intended for meeting the stated objectives; |

| 14 | 322.1 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (6) review the organisational arrangements so that the security concerns are |

| 14 | 342.8 | 128.7 | 523.3 | appreciated, receive adequate attention, and get escalated to appropriate |

| 14 | 363.5 | 128.7 | 367.5 | levels in the hierarchy to enable quick action; |

| 14 | 396.2 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (7) review, at least on an annual basis, the adequacy and effectiveness of the |

| 14 | 416.9 | 128.7 | 523.4 | Business Continuity Planning and Disaster Recovery (DR) Management of |

| 14 | 437.6 | 128.7 | 178.1 | the bank; |

| 14 | 470.3 | 128.7 | 523.3 | (Note: The reference to Business Continuity / Disaster Recovery |

| 14 | 491.0 | 128.7 | 523.3 | Management in these Directions is limited to operational resilience |

| 14 | 511.7 | 128.7 | 523.3 | focussing on People, Processes, and Systems associated with the IT, |

| 14 | 532.4 | 128.7 | 523.3 | Information Systems (IS), information security / cybersecurity controls and |

| 14 | 553.0 | 128.7 | 188.7 | operations) |

| 14 | 585.7 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (8) review the assessment of IT capacity requirements and the measures taken |

| 14 | 606.4 | 128.7 | 268.8 | to address the issues; and |

| 14 | 639.1 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (9) approve documented standards and procedures for administering need- |

| 14 | 659.8 | 128.7 | 339.5 | based access to an information system. |

| 14 | 693.1 | 72.0 | 369.9 | D. Senior Management and IT Steering Committee |

| 14 | 725.2 | 72.0 | 421.9 | 20. The Senior Management of the bank shall, inter alia, ensure: |

| 14 | 757.9 | 100.3 | 403.3 | (1) execution of the IT Strategy approved by the Board; |

| 14 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 14 |

| 15 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 15 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 15 | 79.1 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (2) IT / Information Security and their support infrastructure are functioning |

| 15 | 99.8 | 128.7 | 261.6 | effectively and efficiently; |

| 15 | 132.5 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (3) necessary IT risk management processes are in place and create a culture |

| 15 | 153.2 | 128.7 | 454.0 | of IT risk awareness and cyber hygiene practices in the bank; |

| 15 | 185.9 | 100.3 | 380.9 | (4) cybersecurity posture of the bank is robust; and |

| 15 | 218.6 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (5) overall, IT contributes to productivity, effectiveness, and efficiency in |

| 15 | 239.3 | 128.7 | 238.7 | business operations. |

| 15 | 272.0 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 21. The bank shall establish an IT Steering Committee with representation at Senior |

| 15 | 292.7 | 100.4 | 523.4 | Management level from IT and business functions which shall meet at least on a |

| 15 | 313.4 | 100.4 | 181.7 | quarterly basis. |

| 15 | 346.1 | 72.0 | 456.3 | 22. The responsibilities of IT Steering Committee, inter alia, shall be to: |

| 15 | 378.8 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (1) assist the ITSC in strategic IT planning, oversight of IT performance, and |

| 15 | 399.5 | 128.7 | 347.9 | aligning IT activities with business needs; |

| 15 | 432.2 | 100.3 | 483.6 | (2) oversee the processes put in place for business continuity and DR; |

| 15 | 464.9 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (3) ensure implementation of a robust IT architecture meeting statutory and |

| 15 | 485.6 | 128.7 | 272.8 | regulatory compliance; and |

| 15 | 518.3 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (4) update the ITSC and Managing Director (MD) / Chief Executive Officer |

| 15 | 539.0 | 128.7 | 452.0 | (CEO) periodically on the activities of IT Steering Committee. |

| 15 | 572.3 | 72.0 | 282.4 | E. Information Security Committee |

| 15 | 604.4 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 23. An Information Security Committee (ISC), under the oversight of the ITSC, shall |

| 15 | 625.0 | 100.4 | 523.2 | be formed for managing cyber / information security. The constitution of the ISC, |

| 15 | 645.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | with Chief Information Security Officer (CISO) and other representatives from |

| 15 | 666.4 | 100.4 | 523.3 | business, IT, and other relevant functions, shall be decided by the ITSC. The |

| 15 | 687.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | head of the ISC shall be from risk management vertical. Major responsibilities of |

| 15 | 707.8 | 100.4 | 268.4 | the ISC, inter alia, shall include: |

| 15 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 15 |

| 16 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 16 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 16 | 79.1 | 100.3 | 523.2 | (1) development of information security / cybersecurity policies, |

| 16 | 99.8 | 128.7 | 523.4 | implementation of policies, standards, and procedures to ensure that all |

| 16 | 120.5 | 128.7 | 442.0 | identified risks are managed within the bank’s risk appetite; |

| 16 | 153.2 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (2) approving and monitoring information security / cybersecurity projects and |

| 16 | 173.9 | 128.7 | 287.4 | security awareness initiatives; |

| 16 | 206.6 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (3) reviewing cyber incidents, IS audit observations, monitoring, and mitigation |

| 16 | 227.3 | 128.7 | 201.4 | activities; and |

| 16 | 260.0 | 100.3 | 496.2 | (4) updating the ITSC and MD / CEO periodically on the activities of ISC. |

| 16 | 293.3 | 72.0 | 212.4 | F. Head of IT Function |

| 16 | 325.4 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 24. The bank shall appoint a sufficiently senior level, technically competent, and |

| 16 | 346.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | experienced official in IT related aspects as Head of IT Function (or by whatever |

| 16 | 366.8 | 100.4 | 523.3 | nomenclature the bank uses, such as Chief Technology Officer, Chief Information |

| 16 | 387.5 | 100.4 | 142.8 | Officer). |

| 16 | 420.2 | 72.0 | 485.0 | 25. The Head of IT Function shall, inter alia, be responsible for the following: |

| 16 | 452.9 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (1) ensuring that the execution of IT projects / initiatives is aligned with the |

| 16 | 473.6 | 128.7 | 300.8 | bank’s IT Policy and IT Strategy; |

| 16 | 506.3 | 100.3 | 523.6 | (2) ensuring that there is an effective organisational structure to support IT |

| 16 | 527.0 | 128.7 | 265.5 | functions in the bank; and |

| 16 | 559.7 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (3) putting in place an effective DR setup and business continuity strategy / |

| 16 | 580.4 | 128.7 | 154.7 | plan. |

| 16 | 613.0 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 26. As a first line of defence, the Head of IT Function shall ensure effective |

| 16 | 633.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | assessment, evaluation, and management of IT controls and IT risk, including |

| 16 | 654.4 | 100.4 | 356.6 | the implementation of robust internal controls, to |

| 16 | 687.1 | 100.3 | 349.9 | (1) secure the bank’s information assets; and |

| 16 | 719.8 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (2) comply with extant internal policies, regulatory and legal requirements on |

| 16 | 740.5 | 128.7 | 233.9 | IT related aspects. |

| 17 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 17 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 17 | 79.7 | 72.0 | 292.4 | G. Chief Information Security Officer |

| 17 | 111.8 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 27. A senior level executive (preferably in the rank of a General Manager or an |

| 17 | 132.5 | 100.4 | 523.3 | equivalent position) shall be designated as the CISO. The CISO shall not have |

| 17 | 153.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | any direct reporting relationship with the Head of IT Function and shall not be |

| 17 | 173.9 | 100.4 | 440.3 | given any business targets. The bank shall ensure the following: |

| 17 | 206.6 | 100.3 | 469.0 | (1) The CISO has the requisite technical background and expertise. |

| 17 | 239.3 | 100.3 | 422.9 | (2) The CISO is appointed for a reasonable minimum term. |

| 17 | 272.0 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (3) The CISO’s office is adequately staffed with people having necessary |

| 17 | 292.7 | 128.7 | 523.4 | technical expertise, commensurate with the business volume, extent of |

| 17 | 313.4 | 128.7 | 321.9 | technology adoption and complexity. |

| 17 | 346.1 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (4) The budget for the information security / cybersecurity is determined |

| 17 | 366.8 | 128.7 | 426.8 | keeping in view the current / emerging threat landscape. |

| 17 | 399.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 28. The bank shall ensure that the roles and responsibilities of the CISO are clearly |

| 17 | 420.2 | 100.4 | 471.9 | defined and documented covering, at a minimum, the following points: |

| 17 | 452.9 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (1) The CISO shall be responsible for driving cybersecurity strategy and |

| 17 | 473.6 | 128.7 | 523.3 | ensuring compliance to the extant regulatory / statutory instructions on |

| 17 | 494.3 | 128.7 | 315.8 | information security / cybersecurity. |

| 17 | 527.0 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (2) The CISO shall be responsible for enforcing the policies that a bank uses |

| 17 | 547.7 | 128.7 | 523.3 | to protect its information assets apart from coordinating information |

| 17 | 568.4 | 128.7 | 523.3 | security / cybersecurity related issues within the bank as well as with |

| 17 | 589.0 | 128.7 | 272.1 | relevant external agencies. |

| 17 | 621.7 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (3) The CISO shall be a permanent invitee to the ITSC and IT Steering |

| 17 | 642.4 | 128.7 | 190.1 | Committee. |

| 17 | 675.1 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (4) The CISO’s office shall manage and monitor Security Operations Centre |

| 17 | 695.8 | 128.7 | 378.2 | (SOC) and drive cybersecurity related projects. |

| 17 | 728.5 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (5) The CISO’s office shall ensure effective functioning of the security solutions |

| 17 | 749.2 | 128.7 | 180.7 | deployed. |

| 17 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 17 |

| 18 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 18 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 18 | 79.1 | 100.3 | 523.2 | (6) The CISO shall directly report to the Executive Director or equivalent |

| 18 | 99.8 | 128.7 | 405.5 | executive overseeing the risk management function. |

| 18 | 132.5 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (7) The CISO shall place a review of cybersecurity risks / arrangements / |

| 18 | 153.2 | 128.7 | 523.3 | preparedness of the bank before the Board / Risk Management Committee |

| 18 | 173.9 | 128.7 | 431.5 | of the Board (RMCB) / ITSC at least on a quarterly basis. |

| 18 | 207.2 | 72.0 | 357.8 | H. Information Technology Project Management |

| 18 | 239.3 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 29. The bank shall follow a consistent and formally defined project management |

| 18 | 260.0 | 100.4 | 523.4 | approach for IT projects which shall, inter alia, enable appropriate stakeholder |

| 18 | 280.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | participation for effective monitoring and management of project risks and |

| 18 | 301.4 | 100.4 | 150.4 | progress. |

| 18 | 334.1 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 30. The bank shall adopt a standard enterprise architecture planning methodology |

| 18 | 354.8 | 100.4 | 523.3 | or framework while adopting new or emerging technologies, tools, or while |

| 18 | 375.5 | 100.4 | 316.5 | revamping the existing technology stack. |

| 18 | 408.2 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 31. The bank shall ensure that the adoption of new or emerging technologies is |

| 18 | 428.9 | 100.4 | 523.5 | commensurate with its risk appetite and aligned with the overall business / IT |

| 18 | 449.6 | 100.4 | 523.4 | strategy. The bank shall also ensure that such adoption facilitates the optimal |

| 18 | 470.3 | 100.4 | 455.0 | creation / use / sharing of information in a secure and resilient way. |

| 18 | 503.0 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 32. The bank shall ensure that any new IT application proposed for introduction as a |

| 18 | 523.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | business product undergoes the prescribed product approval and quality |

| 18 | 544.4 | 100.4 | 204.4 | assurance process. |

| 18 | 577.0 | 100.4 | 523.3 | (Note: IT applications that enable functioning of a business process whether |

| 18 | 597.7 | 100.4 | 523.4 | offered as a product to the customers (including potential customers) / third |

| 18 | 618.4 | 100.4 | 501.3 | parties / internal employees could be broadly referred as business product.) |

| 18 | 651.7 | 72.0 | 185.1 | I. IT Architecture |

| 18 | 683.8 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 33. The bank shall design the IT architecture to facilitate security measures being in |

| 18 | 704.5 | 100.4 | 523.2 | place at all times. The IT architecture shall be reviewed by the ITSC at least |

| 18 | 725.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | annually and upgraded, as required, based on the bank’s risk assessment and |

| 19 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 19 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 19 | 79.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | in a phased manner. The bank shall document in writing any risk-cost or potential |

| 19 | 99.8 | 100.4 | 475.7 | cost trade-off decisions to enable appropriate supervisory assessment. |

| 19 | 133.1 | 72.0 | 240.4 | J. IT Services Management |

| 19 | 165.2 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 34. The bank shall put in place a robust IT Service Management Framework for |

| 19 | 185.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | supporting its information systems and infrastructure to ensure the operational |

| 19 | 206.6 | 100.4 | 418.3 | resilience of their entire IT environment (including DR sites). |

| 19 | 239.3 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 35. The bank shall put in place a Service Level Management (SLM) process to |

| 19 | 260.0 | 100.4 | 482.8 | manage the IT operations while ensuring effective segregation of duties. |

| 19 | 292.7 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 36. The bank shall ensure identification and mapping of the security classification (in |

| 19 | 313.4 | 100.4 | 523.4 | terms of confidentiality, integrity, and availability) of information assets based on |

| 19 | 334.1 | 100.4 | 306.9 | their criticality to the bank’s operations. |

| 19 | 366.8 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 37. For seamless continuity of business operations, the bank shall avoid using |

| 19 | 387.5 | 100.4 | 523.2 | outdated and unsupported hardware or software and shall monitor software End- |

| 19 | 408.2 | 100.4 | 523.6 | of-Support (EOS) dates and Annual Maintenance Contract (AMC) dates of IT |

| 19 | 428.9 | 100.4 | 264.5 | hardware on an ongoing basis. |

| 19 | 461.6 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 38. The bank shall develop a technology refresh plan for the replacement of |

| 19 | 482.3 | 100.4 | 452.6 | hardware and software in a timely manner before they reach EOS. |

| 20 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 20 | 79.7 | 131.9 | 463.3 | Chapter IV - IT and Information Security Risk Management |

| 20 | 112.4 | 72.0 | 299.1 | A. Periodic Review of IT related Risks |

| 20 | 144.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 39. The risk management policy of the bank shall include IT related risks, including |

| 20 | 165.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | the cybersecurity related risks. The RMCB in consultation with the ITSC shall |

| 20 | 185.9 | 100.4 | 464.0 | periodically review and update the same at least on an annual basis. |

| 20 | 219.2 | 72.0 | 431.8 | B. IT and Information Security Risk Management Framework |

| 20 | 251.3 | 72.0 | 523.2 | 40. The bank shall establish a robust IT and Information Security Risk Management |

| 20 | 272.0 | 100.4 | 523.4 | Framework (the bank may have flexibility to define information security / |

| 20 | 292.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | cybersecurity risk management framework distinct from IT risk management |

| 20 | 313.4 | 100.4 | 382.5 | framework) covering, inter alia, the following aspects: |

| 20 | 346.1 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (1) implementation of comprehensive information security management |

| 20 | 366.8 | 128.7 | 523.3 | function, internal controls, and processes (including applicable insurance |

| 20 | 387.5 | 128.7 | 523.3 | covers) to mitigate / manage identified risks. The implemented controls and |

| 20 | 408.2 | 128.7 | 523.3 | processes must be reviewed periodically on their efficacy in a risk |

| 20 | 428.9 | 128.7 | 332.1 | environment characterised by change; |

| 20 | 461.6 | 100.3 | 523.2 | (2) roles and responsibilities of stakeholders (including third-party personnel) |

| 20 | 482.3 | 128.7 | 523.4 | involved in IT risk management. Areas of possible role conflicts and |

| 20 | 503.0 | 128.7 | 523.3 | accountability gaps must be specifically identified and eliminated or |

| 20 | 523.7 | 128.7 | 182.0 | managed; |

| 20 | 556.4 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (3) identification of critical information systems of the organisation and |

| 20 | 577.0 | 128.7 | 450.2 | fortification of the security environment of such systems; and |

| 20 | 609.7 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (4) definition and implementation of necessary systems, procedures, and |

| 20 | 630.4 | 128.7 | 523.4 | controls to ensure secure storage / transmission / processing of data / |

| 20 | 651.1 | 128.7 | 191.4 | information. |

| 20 | 684.4 | 72.0 | 399.8 | C. Risk Identification, Assessment, and Documentation |

| 20 | 716.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 41. The risk assessment for each information asset within the bank’s scope shall be |

| 20 | 737.2 | 100.4 | 445.7 | guided by appropriate security standards / IT control frameworks. |

| 20 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 20 |

| 21 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 21 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 21 | 79.1 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 42. The bank shall ensure that all staff members and service providers comply with |

| 21 | 99.8 | 100.4 | 523.4 | the extant information security and acceptable-use policies as applicable to |

| 21 | 120.5 | 100.4 | 130.4 | them. |

| 21 | 153.2 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 43. The bank shall review the security infrastructure and security policies at least |

| 21 | 173.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | annually, factoring in its own experiences and emerging threats and risks. The |

| 21 | 194.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | bank shall take steps to adequately tackle cyber-attacks including phishing, |

| 21 | 215.3 | 100.4 | 370.4 | spoofing attacks and mitigate their adverse effects. |

| 21 | 248.0 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 44. While identifying and assessing the inherent risks, the bank shall reckon the |

| 21 | 268.7 | 100.4 | 523.4 | technologies adopted, alignment with business and regulatory requirements, |

| 21 | 289.4 | 100.4 | 396.5 | organisational culture, and internal and external threats. |

| 21 | 322.1 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 45. Depending on the level of inherent risks, the bank shall identify its riskiness as |

| 21 | 342.8 | 100.4 | 523.2 | low, moderate, high, and very high or adopt any other similar categorisation. |

| 21 | 363.5 | 100.4 | 523.3 | Riskiness of the business component shall also be factored into while assessing |

| 21 | 384.2 | 100.4 | 195.2 | the inherent risks. |

| 21 | 416.9 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 46. While evaluating the controls, the bank shall suitably factor in Board oversight, |

| 21 | 437.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | policies, processes, cyber risk management architecture, availability of |

| 21 | 458.3 | 100.4 | 523.3 | experienced and qualified resources, training programs and organisational |

| 21 | 479.0 | 100.4 | 523.3 | culture; arrangements for threat intelligence gathering, monitoring, and analysing |

| 21 | 499.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | the threat intelligence received vis-à-vis the situation in banking sector; |

| 21 | 520.4 | 100.4 | 523.4 | information sharing arrangements [among peer banks, with Institute for |

| 21 | 541.0 | 100.4 | 523.3 | Development and Research in Banking Technology (IDRBT) / RBI / Indian |

| 21 | 561.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | Computer Emergency Response Team (CERT-In)]; preventive, detective and |

| 21 | 582.4 | 100.4 | 523.3 | corrective cybersecurity controls; vendor management; and incident |

| 21 | 603.1 | 100.4 | 249.8 | management and response. |

| 21 | 635.8 | 100.4 | 523.3 | (Note: For the purpose of these Directions, the term ‘incident’ implies cyber |

| 21 | 656.5 | 100.4 | 145.7 | incident) |

| 21 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 21 |

| 22 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 22 | 79.7 | 112.3 | 483.0 | Chapter V - Baseline Cybersecurity and Resilience Requirements |

| 22 | 112.4 | 72.0 | 357.7 | A. Inventory Management of Information Assets |

| 22 | 144.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 47. The bank shall maintain an up-to-date inventory of information assets, including |

| 22 | 165.2 | 100.4 | 523.6 | business and customer data / information, business applications, supporting IT |

| 22 | 185.9 | 100.4 | 523.4 | infrastructure, key personnel and facilities (such as hardware, software, network |

| 22 | 206.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | devices, and services) indicating their business criticality. The bank may have its |

| 22 | 227.3 | 100.4 | 315.2 | own criteria for identifying critical assets. |

| 22 | 260.0 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 48. The bank shall classify data / information based on its criteria for information |

| 22 | 280.7 | 100.4 | 232.9 | classification / sensitivity. |

| 22 | 313.4 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 49. The bank shall maintain enterprise data dictionary to enable the sharing of data |

| 22 | 334.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | among applications and information systems and promote a common |

| 22 | 354.8 | 100.4 | 219.8 | understanding of data. |

| 22 | 387.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 50. The bank shall appropriately manage and provide protection within and outside |

| 22 | 408.2 | 100.4 | 523.2 | organisation borders / network taking into consideration how the data / |

| 22 | 428.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | information are stored, transmitted, processed, accessed and used within / |

| 22 | 449.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | outside the bank’s network, and level of risk it is exposed to depending on the |

| 22 | 470.3 | 100.4 | 285.1 | sensitivity of the data / information. |

| 22 | 503.6 | 72.0 | 273.7 | B. Data Leak Prevention Strategy |

| 22 | 535.7 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 51. The bank shall develop a comprehensive data loss / leakage prevention strategy |

| 22 | 556.4 | 100.4 | 523.4 | to safeguard sensitive (including confidential) business and customer data / |

| 22 | 577.0 | 100.4 | 523.2 | information. This shall include protecting data processed in end point devices, |

| 22 | 597.7 | 100.4 | 523.2 | data in transmission, as well as data stored in servers and other digital stores, |

| 22 | 618.4 | 100.4 | 230.9 | whether online or offline. |

| 22 | 651.1 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 52. The bank shall ensure similar arrangements at the vendor-managed facilities as |

| 22 | 671.8 | 100.4 | 124.4 | well. |

| 22 | 704.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 53. The bank, as owner of customers’ personal and sensitive data, shall take |

| 22 | 725.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | appropriate steps in preserving the confidentiality, integrity, and availability of the |

| 22 | 745.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | same, irrespective of whether the data is stored / in transit within itself or with the |

| 23 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 23 | 79.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | third-party vendors. The bank shall ensure that the confidentiality of such |

| 23 | 99.8 | 100.4 | 523.4 | custodial information is not compromised at any situation and to this end, shall |

| 23 | 120.5 | 100.4 | 523.3 | put in place suitable systems and processes across the data / information |

| 23 | 141.2 | 100.4 | 146.3 | lifecycle. |

| 23 | 173.9 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 54. The bank shall implement controls for remote management / wiping / locking of |

| 23 | 194.6 | 100.4 | 275.8 | mobile devices including laptops. |

| 23 | 227.9 | 72.0 | 236.4 | C. Data Migration Controls |

| 23 | 260.0 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 55. The bank shall put in place a data migration policy specifying a systematic |

| 23 | 280.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | process for data migration, ensuring data integrity, completeness, and |

| 23 | 301.4 | 100.4 | 523.3 | consistency. The policy shall, inter alia, contain provisions for maintenance of |

| 23 | 322.1 | 100.4 | 523.4 | audit trails of data migration activities and obtaining signoffs from business users |

| 23 | 342.8 | 100.4 | 387.9 | and application owners at each stage of the migration. |

| 23 | 376.1 | 72.0 | 372.4 | D. Preventing Execution of Unauthorised Software |

| 23 | 408.2 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 56. The bank shall maintain an up-to-date and centralised inventory of authorised / |

| 23 | 428.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | unauthorised software(s). The bank may implement whitelisting of authorised |

| 23 | 449.6 | 100.4 | 491.9 | applications / software / libraries and other relevant software components. |

| 23 | 482.3 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 57. The bank shall have a mechanism to control installation of software / applications |

| 23 | 503.0 | 100.4 | 523.3 | centrally / otherwise on its information systems including end-user Personal |

| 23 | 523.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | Computers (PCs), laptops, workstations, servers, mobile devices, and cloud |

| 23 | 544.4 | 100.4 | 523.3 | environments. The bank shall also have a mechanism to block / prevent and |

| 23 | 565.0 | 100.4 | 523.4 | identify installation and running of unauthorised software / applications on such |

| 23 | 585.7 | 100.4 | 198.5 | devices / systems. |

| 23 | 618.4 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 58. The bank shall continuously monitor the release of patches by various vendors / |

| 23 | 639.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | Original Equipment Manufacturers (OEMs), advisories issued by CERT-In and |

| 23 | 659.8 | 100.4 | 523.4 | other similar agencies and expeditiously apply the security patches as per the |

| 23 | 680.5 | 100.4 | 523.3 | patch management policy of the bank. Where a patch / series of patches is / are |

| 23 | 701.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | released by the OEM / manufacturer / vendor to mitigate critical vulnerabilities |

| 23 | 721.9 | 100.4 | 523.4 | that are actively exploited or widely reported, the bank shall have a mechanism |

| 24 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 24 | 79.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | to deploy such patches expeditiously, in accordance with its emergency patch |

| 24 | 99.8 | 100.4 | 219.2 | management process. |

| 24 | 132.5 | 72.0 | 523.2 | 59. The bank shall put in place a policy specifying the justification for exceptions, |

| 24 | 153.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | duration of exceptions, process for granting exceptions, approving authority, and |

| 24 | 173.9 | 100.4 | 523.4 | periodic review of granted exceptions by officers, at senior levels, who are |

| 24 | 194.6 | 100.4 | 519.3 | equipped to understand the business and technical context of such exceptions. |

| 24 | 227.9 | 72.0 | 313.1 | E. Physical and Environmental Controls |

| 24 | 260.0 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 60. The bank shall put in place appropriate physical and environmental controls for |

| 24 | 280.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | securing location of critical assets including Data Centre (DC) and Disaster |

| 24 | 301.4 | 100.4 | 402.5 | Recovery (DR) sites from natural and man-made threats. |

| 24 | 334.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | (Note: DC refers to primary data centre for a given application / system and DR |

| 24 | 354.8 | 100.4 | 369.2 | refers to its Disaster Recovery site / alternate site.) |

| 24 | 387.5 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 61. The bank shall put in place mechanisms for monitoring of breaches of |

| 24 | 408.2 | 100.4 | 523.4 | environmental controls including temperature, water, smoke, access alarms, |

| 24 | 428.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | service availability alerts (power supply, telecommunication, servers), and |

| 24 | 449.6 | 100.4 | 166.4 | access logs. |

| 24 | 482.3 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 62. The DC and DR sites shall be geographically well separated so that both the |

| 24 | 503.0 | 100.4 | 458.3 | sites are not affected by a similar threat associated to their location. |

| 24 | 535.7 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 63. The bank shall ensure that DC and DR sites are subjected to necessary e- |

| 24 | 556.4 | 100.4 | 231.8 | surveillance mechanism. |

| 24 | 589.6 | 72.0 | 227.0 | F. Capacity Management |

| 24 | 621.7 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 64. The bank shall ensure that information systems and infrastructure are able to |

| 24 | 642.4 | 100.4 | 523.3 | support business functions and ensure availability of all service delivery |

| 24 | 663.1 | 100.4 | 151.7 | channels. |

| 24 | 695.8 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 65. On an annual or more frequent basis, the bank shall proactively assess capacity |

| 24 | 716.5 | 100.4 | 523.3 | requirement of IT resources. The bank shall ensure that IT capacity planning |

| 24 | 737.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | across components, services, system resources, supporting infrastructure is |

| 25 | 79.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | consistent with past trends (peak usage), the current business requirements, and |

| 25 | 99.8 | 100.4 | 402.3 | projected future needs as per the IT strategy of the bank. |

| 25 | 133.1 | 72.0 | 222.4 | G. Secure Configuration |

| 25 | 165.2 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 66. The bank shall document and apply baseline security requirements / |

| 25 | 185.9 | 100.4 | 523.2 | configurations to all categories of devices including endpoints / workstations, |

| 25 | 206.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | mobile devices, operating systems, databases, applications, network devices, |

| 25 | 227.3 | 100.4 | 523.3 | security devices, and security systems, throughout the lifecycle (from conception |

| 25 | 248.0 | 100.4 | 362.9 | to deployment) and carry out reviews periodically. |

| 25 | 280.7 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 67. The bank shall put in place a mechanism to periodically evaluate and review |

| 25 | 301.4 | 100.4 | 523.3 | critical devices such as firewall, network switches and security devices, including |

| 25 | 322.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | their configurations and patch levels, across the bank’s network including data |

| 25 | 342.8 | 100.4 | 462.6 | centres, third-party hosted sites, and shared-infrastructure locations. |

| 25 | 376.1 | 72.0 | 299.8 | H. Network Management and Security |

| 25 | 408.2 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 68. The bank shall ensure that only authorised access is permitted to its networks. |

| 25 | 428.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | Wherever the access is permitted, the bank shall ensure that it is provided |

| 25 | 449.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | through well-defined process with strict time limits, which are consistently |

| 25 | 470.3 | 100.4 | 523.3 | followed and audited periodically. The bank shall clearly elucidate responsibility |

| 25 | 491.0 | 100.4 | 446.9 | over such networks, which shall invariably rest with bank officials. |

| 25 | 523.7 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 69. The bank shall prepare and maintain an up-to-date network architecture diagram |

| 25 | 544.4 | 100.4 | 417.9 | at the organisation level including wired / wireless networks. |

| 25 | 577.0 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 70. The bank shall maintain an up to date / centralised inventory of authorised |

| 25 | 597.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | devices connected to the bank’s network (within / outside bank’s premises). The |

| 25 | 618.4 | 100.4 | 523.4 | bank may consider implementing solutions to automate network discovery and |

| 25 | 639.1 | 100.4 | 173.8 | management. |

| 25 | 671.8 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 71. The bank shall ensure that all the network devices are configured appropriately. |

| 25 | 692.5 | 100.4 | 523.4 | The bank shall periodically assess whether the configurations are appropriate to |

| 25 | 713.2 | 100.4 | 293.6 | the desired level of network security. |

| 26 | 79.1 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 72. The bank shall put in place appropriate controls to secure wireless local area |

| 26 | 99.8 | 100.4 | 470.5 | networks, wireless access points, and wireless client access systems. |

| 26 | 132.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 73. The bank shall implement mechanism to identify authorised hardware / mobile |

| 26 | 153.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | devices such as laptops, mobile phones, and tablets, in the network and ensure |

| 26 | 173.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | that they are provided connectivity only when they meet the security |

| 26 | 194.6 | 100.4 | 298.5 | requirements prescribed by the bank. |

| 26 | 227.3 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 74. The bank shall have mechanism to automatically identify unauthorised device |

| 26 | 248.0 | 100.4 | 435.0 | connections to the bank’s network and block such connections. |

| 26 | 280.7 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 75. The bank shall put in place mechanism to detect and remedy any unusual |

| 26 | 301.4 | 100.4 | 430.5 | activities in systems, servers, network devices, and endpoints. |

| 26 | 334.1 | 72.0 | 523.6 | 76. The bank shall establish Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs) for all major IT |

| 26 | 354.8 | 100.4 | 401.9 | activities including for connecting devices to the network. |

| 26 | 387.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 77. The bank shall ensure that boundary defences are multi-layered with properly |

| 26 | 408.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | configured firewalls, proxies, DMZ perimeter networks, and network-based |

| 26 | 428.9 | 100.4 | 523.4 | Intrusion Prevention System (IPS) and Intrusion Detection System (IDS). |

| 26 | 449.6 | 100.4 | 523.4 | Additionally, the bank shall establish robust mechanism for real-time filtering of |

| 26 | 470.3 | 100.4 | 301.0 | inbound and outbound network traffic. |

| 26 | 503.0 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 78. The bank shall enable its public facing Information technology (IT) infrastructure |

| 26 | 523.7 | 100.4 | 365.6 | to handle Internet Protocol Version 6 (IPv6) traffic. |

| 26 | 557.0 | 72.0 | 276.5 | I. Application Security Life Cycle |

| 26 | 589.0 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 79. The bank shall incorporate / ensure information security across all stages of |

| 26 | 609.7 | 100.4 | 210.4 | application life cycle. |

| 26 | 642.4 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 80. The bank shall implement secure coding practices for internally / collaboratively |

| 26 | 663.1 | 100.4 | 225.8 | developed applications. |

| 26 | 695.8 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 81. Besides business functionalities, the bank shall clearly specify security |

| 26 | 716.5 | 100.4 | 523.3 | requirements relating to system access control, authentication, transaction |

| 26 | 737.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | authorisation, data integrity, system activity logging, audit trail, session |

| 27 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 27 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 27 | 79.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | management, security event tracking, and exception handling during the initial |

| 27 | 99.8 | 100.4 | 493.2 | and ongoing stages of system development / acquisition / implementation. |

| 27 | 132.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 82. The bank shall properly segregate the development, test, and production |

| 27 | 153.2 | 100.4 | 175.8 | environments. |

| 27 | 185.9 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 83. The bank shall ensure that the software / application development process is |

| 27 | 206.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | guided by threat modelling, adheres to secure coding practices, includes security |

| 27 | 227.3 | 100.4 | 484.7 | testing aligned with global standards, and ensures a secure deployment. |

| 27 | 260.0 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 84. The bank shall ensure that software / application development practices address |

| 27 | 280.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | the vulnerabilities based on best practices, baselines such as Open Web |

| 27 | 301.4 | 100.4 | 523.3 | Application Security Project (OWASP) proactively and adopt principle of defence- |

| 27 | 322.1 | 100.4 | 355.2 | in-depth to provide layered security mechanism. |

| 27 | 354.8 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 85. The scope of application security assessments shall be comprehensive and shall |

| 27 | 375.5 | 100.4 | 523.3 | not be restricted to testing solely against the OWASP top 10 vulnerabilities. The |

| 27 | 396.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | bank shall also refer to other recognised security standards and guidelines, as |

| 27 | 416.9 | 100.4 | 164.4 | appropriate. |

| 27 | 449.6 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 86. The bank shall periodically conduct application security testing of web / mobile |

| 27 | 470.3 | 100.4 | 523.3 | applications throughout their lifecycle (pre-implementation, post implementation, |

| 27 | 491.0 | 100.4 | 523.3 | and after changes) in an environment closely resembling or a replica of the |

| 27 | 511.7 | 100.4 | 229.2 | production environment. |

| 27 | 544.4 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 87. The bank shall implement measures such as installing ‘containerised’ apps on |

| 27 | 565.0 | 100.4 | 523.4 | mobile / smartphones for exclusive business use that is encrypted and separated |

| 27 | 585.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | from other smartphone data / applications, as applicable. The bank shall |

| 27 | 606.4 | 100.4 | 523.3 | implement measures to initiate a remote wipe on the containerised app, |

| 27 | 627.1 | 100.4 | 389.9 | rendering the data unreadable, in case of requirement. |

| 27 | 659.8 | 72.0 | 523.2 | 88. The bank shall ensure adequate evaluation of adoption of new technologies for |

| 27 | 680.5 | 100.4 | 523.3 | existing / evolving security threats. Such technologies shall be introduced for |

| 27 | 701.2 | 100.4 | 523.4 | critical systems after IT and security team of the bank reach reasonable level of |

| 27 | 721.9 | 100.4 | 212.3 | comfort and maturity. |

| 27 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 27 |

| 28 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 28 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 28 | 79.1 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 89. The bank shall ensure that software vendors provide maintenance and |

| 28 | 99.8 | 100.4 | 523.3 | necessary support of software applications and shall enforce the same through |

| 28 | 120.5 | 100.4 | 203.7 | formal agreements. |

| 28 | 153.2 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 90. The bank shall obtain the source codes for all critical applications from its |

| 28 | 173.9 | 100.4 | 523.4 | vendors. Where obtaining of the source code is not possible, the bank shall put |

| 28 | 194.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | in place a source code escrow arrangement or other arrangements to adequately |

| 28 | 215.3 | 100.4 | 523.4 | mitigate the risk of default by the vendor. The bank shall ensure that all product |

| 28 | 236.0 | 100.4 | 523.3 | updates and programme fixes are included in the source code escrow |

| 28 | 256.7 | 100.4 | 171.8 | arrangement. |

| 28 | 289.4 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 91. In respect of critical business applications, the bank may consider conducting |

| 28 | 310.1 | 100.4 | 513.2 | source code audits by professionally competent personnel / service providers. |

| 28 | 342.8 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 92. The bank shall obtain a certificate or a written confirmation from the application |

| 28 | 363.5 | 100.4 | 523.3 | developer or vendor stating that the application is free of known vulnerabilities, |

| 28 | 384.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | malware, and any covert channels in the code. The bank shall also obtain such |

| 28 | 404.9 | 100.4 | 523.4 | a certificate or a written confirmation whenever material changes to the code, |

| 28 | 425.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | including upgrades, occur. The definition of material changes can be mutually |

| 28 | 446.3 | 100.4 | 523.3 | agreed upon between the bank and its vendor. 'Material Changes' refer to |

| 28 | 467.0 | 100.4 | 523.3 | significant modifications to the software code that could impact the application's |

| 28 | 487.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | functionality, security, or performance. This, however, excludes routine |

| 28 | 508.4 | 100.4 | 523.3 | maintenance activities such as minor bug fixes, performance optimisations, and |

| 28 | 529.0 | 100.4 | 523.4 | updates that do not alter the application's core functions, security posture, or |

| 28 | 549.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | compliance status. It is essential to conduct a risk assessment when determining |

| 28 | 570.4 | 100.4 | 257.1 | whether a change is material. |

| 28 | 603.7 | 72.0 | 403.0 | J. Maintenance, Monitoring, and Analysis of Audit Logs |

| 28 | 635.8 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 93. The bank shall ensure that every IT application / system that can access or affect |

| 28 | 656.5 | 100.4 | 523.3 | critical or sensitive information has necessary audit logging capabilities and |

| 28 | 677.2 | 100.4 | 207.1 | provides audit trails. |

| 28 | 709.9 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 94. The bank shall implement and periodically validate settings for capturing |

| 28 | 730.6 | 100.4 | 523.4 | appropriate logs and audit trails for each device, system software, and |

| 28 | 751.3 | 100.4 | 523.3 | application software, ensuring that such logs include the minimum information |

| 28 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 28 |

| 29 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 29 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 29 | 79.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | required to uniquely identify each log, such as date, timestamp, source |

| 29 | 99.8 | 100.4 | 523.3 | addresses, destination addresses, and other relevant elements of each packet, |

| 29 | 120.5 | 100.4 | 242.4 | event, and / or transaction. |

| 29 | 153.2 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 95. The bank shall ensure that audit trails satisfy its business requirements, in |

| 29 | 173.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | addition to regulatory and legal requirements. The bank shall also ensure that |

| 29 | 194.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | audit trails are sufficiently detailed to facilitate the conduct of audits, serve as |

| 29 | 215.3 | 100.4 | 523.4 | forensic evidence (the term ‘forensics’ refers to ‘digital forensics’ for the purpose |

| 29 | 236.0 | 100.4 | 523.4 | of these Directions) when required, and assist in dispute resolution, including for |

| 29 | 256.7 | 100.4 | 239.8 | non-repudiation purposes. |

| 29 | 289.4 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 96. The bank shall put in place a system for regularly monitoring the audit trails and |

| 29 | 310.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | system logs to detect, understand or recover from any unauthorised activity or |

| 29 | 330.8 | 100.4 | 135.8 | attack. |

| 29 | 363.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 97. The bank shall consult all the stakeholders before finalising the scope, frequency, |

| 29 | 384.2 | 100.4 | 253.1 | and storage of log collection. |

| 29 | 417.5 | 72.0 | 360.4 | K. Patch, Vulnerability and Change Management |

| 29 | 449.6 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 98. The bank shall put in place documented policy(ies) and procedures for change |

| 29 | 470.3 | 100.4 | 353.2 | and patch management to ensure the following: |

| 29 | 503.0 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (1) The business impact of implementing patches / changes (or not |

| 29 | 523.7 | 128.7 | 461.5 | implementing a particular patch / change request) is assessed. |

| 29 | 556.4 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (2) The patches / changes are applied / implemented and reviewed in a secure |

| 29 | 577.0 | 128.7 | 368.8 | and timely manner with necessary approvals. |

| 29 | 609.7 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (3) Any changes to an application system or data are justified by genuine |

| 29 | 630.4 | 128.7 | 523.3 | business needs and approvals supported by documentation and subjected |

| 29 | 651.1 | 128.7 | 350.2 | to a robust change management process. |

| 29 | 683.8 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (4) A mechanism is established to recover from failed changes / patch |

| 29 | 704.5 | 128.7 | 312.2 | deployment or unexpected results. |

| 29 | 737.2 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 99. The bank shall adopt a documented, risk-based strategy for maintaining an |

| 29 | 757.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | inventory of IT components that require patching, identifying all applicable |

| 29 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 29 |

| 30 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 30 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 30 | 79.1 | 100.4 | 523.2 | patches, and applying them in a timely manner to minimise both the number of |

| 30 | 99.8 | 100.4 | 362.5 | vulnerable systems and the duration of exposure. |

| 30 | 132.5 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 100. The bank shall put in place systems and processes to identify, track, manage, |

| 30 | 153.2 | 100.4 | 523.4 | and monitor the status of patches to operating system and application software |

| 30 | 173.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | running at end-user devices directly connected to the internet and in respect of |

| 30 | 194.6 | 100.4 | 523.4 | server operating systems, databases, applications, middleware, and other |

| 30 | 215.3 | 100.4 | 256.5 | relevant information systems. |

| 30 | 248.0 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 101. The bank shall manage changes to business applications, supporting |

| 30 | 268.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | technology, service components, and facilities through robust configuration |

| 30 | 289.4 | 100.4 | 523.4 | management processes and configuration baselines to ensure the integrity of |

| 30 | 310.1 | 100.4 | 177.8 | such changes. |

| 30 | 342.8 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 102. As part of its threat mitigation strategy, the bank shall determine the root cause |

| 30 | 363.5 | 100.4 | 523.3 | of any detected incident and implement necessary patches to address the |

| 30 | 384.2 | 100.4 | 176.4 | vulnerabilities. |

| 30 | 416.9 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 103. The bank shall evaluate the access device configurations and patch levels |

| 30 | 437.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | periodically to ensure that all access points, nodes between (i) different Virtual |

| 30 | 458.3 | 100.4 | 523.3 | Local Area Networks (VLANs) in the DC, (ii) Local Area Network (LAN) / Wide |

| 30 | 479.0 | 100.4 | 523.3 | Area Network (WAN) interfaces, (iii) bank’s network to external network and |

| 30 | 499.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | interconnections with partner, vendor and service provider networks are securely |

| 30 | 520.4 | 100.4 | 159.7 | configured. |

| 30 | 553.6 | 72.0 | 301.7 | L. User Access Control / Management |

| 30 | 585.7 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 104. Access to information assets shall be allowed only where a valid business need |

| 30 | 606.4 | 100.4 | 134.4 | exists. |

| 30 | 639.1 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 105. Personnel with elevated system access entitlements shall be closely supervised |

| 30 | 659.8 | 100.4 | 435.1 | with all their system activities logged and periodically reviewed. |

| 30 | 692.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 106. The bank shall provide secure access to its assets / services from within / outside |

| 30 | 713.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | its network by protecting data / information at rest (e.g., using encryption, if |

| 30 | 733.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | supported by the device) and in-transit (e.g., using technologies such as Virtual |

| 30 | 754.6 | 100.4 | 361.1 | Private Network (VPN) or other secure protocols) |

| 30 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 30 |

| 31 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 31 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 31 | 79.1 | 72.0 | 523.2 | 107. The bank shall protect user access credentials such as logon user-id, |

| 31 | 99.8 | 100.4 | 523.3 | authentication information and tokens, access profiles, against leakage / attacks. |

| 31 | 132.5 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 108. The bank shall disallow administrative rights on end-user workstations / PCs / |

| 31 | 153.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | laptops and provide access rights on a need-to-know basis and for specific |

| 31 | 173.9 | 100.4 | 423.9 | duration when it is required following an established process. |

| 31 | 206.6 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 109. The bank shall implement centralised authentication and authorisation system |

| 31 | 227.3 | 100.4 | 523.4 | for accessing and administering applications, operating systems, databases, |

| 31 | 248.0 | 100.4 | 523.3 | network and security devices / systems, point of connectivity (local / remote) |

| 31 | 268.7 | 100.4 | 523.2 | including enforcement of strong password policy, and following the principle of |

| 31 | 289.4 | 100.4 | 314.4 | least privileges and separation of duties. |

| 31 | 322.1 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 110. The bank, based on the risk assessment, shall implement two-factor or multi- |

| 31 | 342.8 | 100.4 | 523.3 | factor authentication, including mandatory multi-factor authentication for |

| 31 | 363.5 | 100.4 | 489.0 | privileged users of (i) critical information systems and (ii) critical activities. |

| 31 | 396.2 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 111. The bank shall implement appropriate (e.g., centralised) systems and controls to |

| 31 | 416.9 | 100.4 | 523.4 | allow, manage, log, and monitor privileged / superuser / administrative access to |

| 31 | 437.6 | 100.4 | 523.2 | critical systems (such as servers, operating systems, databases, applications, |

| 31 | 458.3 | 100.4 | 281.8 | middleware and network devices). |

| 31 | 491.0 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 112. The bank shall implement controls to minimise invalid logon counts and |

| 31 | 511.7 | 100.4 | 257.1 | deactivate dormant accounts. |

| 31 | 544.4 | 72.0 | 443.3 | 113. The bank shall monitor any abnormal change in pattern of logon. |

| 31 | 577.6 | 72.0 | 241.0 | M. Controls on Teleworking |

| 31 | 609.7 | 72.0 | 399.9 | 114. In the teleworking environment, the bank shall, inter alia: |

| 31 | 642.4 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (1) ensure that the systems used and the remote access from alternate work |

| 31 | 663.1 | 128.7 | 516.7 | location to the environment hosting bank’s information assets are secure; |

| 31 | 695.8 | 100.3 | 523.4 | (2) implement multi-factor authentication for enterprise access (logical) to |

| 31 | 716.5 | 128.7 | 213.4 | critical systems; |

| 31 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 31 |

| 32 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 32 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 32 | 79.1 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (3) put in place a mechanism to identify all remote-access devices attached / |

| 32 | 99.8 | 128.7 | 329.2 | connected to the bank’s systems; and |

| 32 | 132.5 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (4) ensure that data / information shared / presented in teleworking is secured |

| 32 | 153.2 | 128.7 | 200.5 | appropriately. |

| 32 | 186.5 | 72.0 | 337.0 | N. Authentication Framework for Customers |

| 32 | 218.6 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 115. The bank shall implement an authentication framework to ensure positive identity |

| 32 | 239.3 | 100.4 | 523.3 | verification for customers accessing its services and to enable customers to |

| 32 | 260.0 | 100.4 | 432.5 | verify the authenticity of the bank, across all delivery channels. |

| 32 | 292.7 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 116. The bank shall act as the identity provider for identification and authentication of |

| 32 | 313.4 | 100.4 | 523.3 | customers for access to partner systems using secure authentication |

| 32 | 334.1 | 100.4 | 171.1 | technologies. |

| 32 | 367.4 | 72.0 | 309.8 | O. Secure Mail and Messaging Systems |

| 32 | 399.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 117. The bank shall implement secure mail and messaging systems with measures to |

| 32 | 420.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | prevent email spoofing, look-alike domain abuse, protect against malicious |

| 32 | 440.9 | 100.4 | 523.3 | attachments and links, and email filtering to protect against spam. The bank shall |

| 32 | 461.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | require its partners and vendors to implement similar measures for their mail and |

| 32 | 482.3 | 100.4 | 209.1 | messaging systems. |

| 32 | 515.0 | 72.0 | 477.2 | 118. The bank shall document and implement email server specific controls. |

| 32 | 547.7 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 119. The bank shall implement measures to control permissible attachment types in |

| 32 | 568.4 | 100.4 | 179.7 | email systems. |

| 32 | 601.0 | 72.0 | 523.2 | 120. The bank shall implement Domain-based Message Authentication, Reporting, |

| 32 | 621.7 | 100.4 | 523.3 | and Conformance (DMARC) solution for the email domains. The bank shall |

| 32 | 642.4 | 100.4 | 523.3 | impress upon its vendors, partners, and other relevant third parties to implement |

| 32 | 663.1 | 100.4 | 470.5 | similar solution securing their mail systems to mitigate email spoofing. |

| 32 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 32 |

| 33 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 33 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 33 | 79.7 | 72.0 | 201.8 | P. Removable Media |

| 33 | 111.8 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 121. As default rule, the bank shall not permit use of removable devices and media in |

| 33 | 132.5 | 100.4 | 523.3 | the banking environment unless specifically authorised for defined use and |

| 33 | 153.2 | 100.4 | 183.1 | duration of use. |

| 33 | 185.9 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 122. The bank shall define and implement policy for restriction and secure use of |

| 33 | 206.6 | 100.4 | 523.3 | removable media / Bring Your Own Device (BYOD) on various types / categories |

| 33 | 227.3 | 100.4 | 523.3 | of devices including but not limited to workstations, PCs, laptops, mobile devices, |

| 33 | 248.0 | 100.4 | 464.6 | servers, and ensure secure erasure of data on such media after use. |

| 33 | 280.7 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 123. The bank shall limit media types and information that could be transferred / |

| 33 | 301.4 | 100.4 | 259.1 | copied to / from such devices. |

| 33 | 334.1 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 124. The bank shall get the removable media scanned for malware / anti-virus prior to |

| 33 | 354.8 | 100.4 | 255.8 | providing read / write access. |

| 33 | 387.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 125. The bank shall consider implementing centralised policies through Active |

| 33 | 408.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | Directory or end-point management systems to whitelist / blacklist / restrict |

| 33 | 428.9 | 100.4 | 218.4 | removable media use. |

| 33 | 462.2 | 72.0 | 249.1 | Q. Third-Party Arrangements |

| 33 | 494.3 | 100.4 | 523.3 | (The paragraphs from 126 to 135 are applicable only for such third-party |

| 33 | 515.0 | 100.4 | 523.3 | arrangements in the Information Technology / Cybersecurity ecosystem, which |

| 33 | 535.7 | 100.4 | 523.4 | are not within the applicability of Reserve Bank of India (Commercial Banks – |

| 33 | 556.4 | 100.4 | 364.4 | Managing Risks in Outsourcing) Directions, 2025) |

| 33 | 589.0 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 126. The bank shall put in place appropriate vendor risk assessment process, and |

| 33 | 609.7 | 100.4 | 476.0 | controls proportionate to the assessed risk and materiality to, inter alia: |

| 33 | 642.4 | 100.3 | 271.5 | (1) mitigate concentration risk; |

| 33 | 675.1 | 100.3 | 365.4 | (2) eliminate or address any conflict of interests; |

| 33 | 707.8 | 100.3 | 400.8 | (3) mitigate risks associated with single point of failure; |

| 33 | 807.4 | 292.1 | 303.2 | 33 |

| 34 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 34 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 34 | 79.1 | 100.3 | 523.3 | (4) comply with applicable legal, regulatory requirements and standards to |

| 34 | 99.8 | 128.7 | 248.1 | protect customer data; |

| 34 | 132.5 | 100.3 | 472.9 | (5) provide high availability (for uninterrupted customer service); and |

| 34 | 165.2 | 100.3 | 329.0 | (6) manage supply chain risks effectively. |

| 34 | 197.9 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 127. The bank shall be accountable for ensuring appropriate management and |

| 34 | 218.6 | 100.4 | 465.9 | assurance on security risks in outsourced and partner arrangements. |

| 34 | 251.3 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 128. The bank shall carefully evaluate the need for outsourcing critical processes and |

| 34 | 272.0 | 100.4 | 479.3 | selection of vendor / partner based on comprehensive risk assessment. |

| 34 | 304.7 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 129. The bank shall regularly conduct effective due diligence, oversight, and |

| 34 | 325.4 | 100.4 | 461.9 | management of third-party vendors / service providers and partners. |

| 34 | 358.1 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 130. The bank shall establish appropriate policies and procedures to evaluate, |

| 34 | 378.8 | 100.4 | 523.3 | assess, approve, review, control and monitor the risks and materiality of all its |

| 34 | 399.5 | 100.4 | 261.8 | vendor / outsourcing activities. |

| 34 | 432.2 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 131. The bank shall ensure and demonstrate that the service provider adheres to all |

| 34 | 452.9 | 100.4 | 357.7 | regulatory and legal requirements of the country. |

| 34 | 485.6 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 132. The bank shall necessarily enter into an agreement with the service provider that |

| 34 | 506.3 | 100.4 | 523.3 | amongst others provides for right to audit by the bank and right of RBI or persons |

| 34 | 527.0 | 100.4 | 300.5 | authorised by it to perform inspection. |

| 34 | 559.7 | 72.0 | 523.4 | 133. The bank shall ensure that RBI shall have access to all information resources |

| 34 | 580.4 | 100.4 | 523.3 | (online / in person) that are consumed by it. Such information shall be made |

| 34 | 601.0 | 100.4 | 523.4 | accessible to RBI officials by the bank when sought, though the infrastructure / |

| 34 | 621.7 | 100.4 | 492.6 | enabling resources may not be physically located in the premises of bank. |

| 34 | 654.4 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 134. The bank shall adhere to the relevant legal and regulatory requirements relating |

| 34 | 675.1 | 100.4 | 510.6 | to geographical location of infrastructure and cross-border movement of data. |

| 34 | 707.8 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 135. The bank shall thoroughly satisfy itself about the credentials of vendor / third- |

| 34 | 728.5 | 100.4 | 523.4 | party personnel accessing and managing the bank’s critical assets. Background |

| 35 | 31.2 | 286.1 | 523.2 | RBI (CBs – Cybersecurity, Technology: Risk, Resilience |

| 35 | 41.5 | 334.1 | 523.3 | and Assurance Framework) Directions, 2026 |

| 35 | 79.1 | 100.4 | 523.3 | checks, non-disclosure and security policy compliance agreements shall be |

| 35 | 99.8 | 100.4 | 341.1 | mandated for all third-party service providers. |

| 35 | 132.5 | 72.0 | 523.3 | 136. The bank shall ensure that the following cybersecurity controls are implemented |

| 35 | 153.2 | 100.4 | 523.3 | and maintained by third-party Automated Teller Machine (ATM) Switch |
